16	/// Settings' sections for the onboarding answers: About you, Caffeine and your body, and Bedtime.
17	///
18	/// The factors and the starting half-life come only from the profile the repository publishes, and an option saves at
19	/// once (constitution Article I.5). The name, age, and bedtime are the sections' own editing state (Article I.2).
20	/// They're refilled from every published profile, except a name being typed, and except while a save is under way, so
21	/// a wheel being turned never jumps back. The name saves when it's committed, and a newline typed in it commits it,
22	/// because the field wraps and Return types one. The age and the bedtime save as soon as they're chosen. A failed save
23	/// puts the fields back to what was last published. See the Settings article, SETPROF-1 to SETPROF-7.
24	@Reducer nonisolated struct ProfileSettingsFeature {
25	    private static let logger = Logger(for: ProfileSettingsFeature.self)
26	
27	    /// The sections' state.
28	    @ObservableState
29	    struct State: Equatable {
30	        /// The profile the repository last published, or `nil` until it arrives.

32	        /// The current year in the user's calendar, or `nil` until the time of day arrives.
33	        var currentYear: Int?
34	        /// The name as the field shows it.
35	        var name = ""
36	        /// The age the picker shows, or `nil` for "Prefer not to say".
37	        var age: Int?
38	        /// The bedtime the picker shows.
39	        var bedtime: Bedtime = .standard

22	    private var screen: XCUIElement { app.descendants(matching: .any)[AboutYouSettingsViewAccessibilityID.screen] }
23	    /// The name field wraps, so it may be a text view rather than a text field.
24	    private var nameField: XCUIElement {
25	        app.descendants(matching: .any)[AboutYouSettingsViewAccessibilityID.nameField]
26	    }
27	
28	    /// Replaces the name with `name`, and presses Return, which saves it. The field wraps, so Return types a newline,
29	    /// which the app takes as committing the name.
30	    func enterName(_ name: String, file: StaticString = #filePath, line: UInt = #line) {
31	        reveal(nameField, on: screen, "The name field", file: file, line: line)
32	        nameField.tap()
33	        let typed = (nameField.value as? String) ?? ""
34	        nameField.typeText(String(repeating: XCUIKeyboardKey.delete.rawValue, count: typed.count) + name + "\n")
35	    }
36	}
37	
```

11	The owner runs several Claude Code sessions at the same time against the same working tree (on 2026-09-12: one-tap log, "Today"/"Last cup" tiles, drink history, onboarding). Some work in isolated snapshots and merge back 3-way; others edit the main tree directly. Commits take the whole working tree.
12	
13	**Why:** parallel feature work during the one-week case study (see [[no-exports]] for other working rules).
14	
15	**How to apply:**
16	- Build and test with your own `-derivedDataPath build/<name>` and your own simulator (`xcrun simctl create HalfLife-<task> …`), boot it before the first test run, and pass `-parallel-testing-enabled NO` for reliable runs.
17	- Re-read shared files (TodayFeature/View, AppView, robots, `Localizable.xcstrings`, `project.pbxproj`, DocC articles, `ai_log.md`) right before editing; others change them mid-task.
18	- Another session's half-done code can break the build for a while; check whose file the error is in before assuming it's yours.
19	- Peers message through SendMessage; tell them which shared files you touch and when you're done, and reply to their `from` address.
20	- Another session's half-done UI-test code often stops `xcodebuild test` before unit tests run. A personal scheme in `Half-Life.xcodeproj/xcuserdata/adamure.xcuserdatad/xcschemes/` (git-ignored) with only the app and `Half-LifeTests` keeps TDD cycles moving; the full run still uses the shared scheme.
21	- `project.pbxproj` edits: quote any value with characters like `+` (e.g. `"Logging/Logger+HalfLife.swift"`), or the whole project stops opening for every session; check with `xcodebuild -list` right after writing.
22	- `docbuild` builds docs for every project target, including `Half-LifeWidgets`; check each target's `*-diagnostics.json`, not only the app's.

9	// Half-Life SaveAboutYouUseCase
10	//
11	
12	import Foundation
13	
14	/// Saves the name and age the user gave in onboarding's About you step.
15	///
16	/// The name is trimmed, and a blank one is saved as none. The age is saved as the birth year it implies: the
17	/// current year, in the given calendar, minus the age. See the Onboarding article.
18	nonisolated struct SaveAboutYouUseCase: UseCase {
19	    /// What the user entered.
20	    struct Input: Sendable, Equatable {
21	        /// The name as typed, or `nil`.
22	        let name: String?
23	        /// The age in years, or `nil` if the user preferred not to say.
24	        let age: Int?
25	        /// The calendar whose current year the age is counted back from.
26	        let calendar: Calendar
27	    }
28	
29	    /// The repository that supplies the current time.
30	    let currentTime: any CurrentTimeRepository
31	    /// The repository that stores the profile.
32	    let profile: any UserProfileRepository
33	
34	    /// Saves the trimmed name and the birth year the age implies.
35	    ///
36	    /// - Parameter input: What the user entered.
37	    /// - Throws: The repository's error if the profile couldn't be stored.
38	    func execute(_ input: Input) async throws {
39	        let trimmed = input.name?.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines) ?? ""
40	        let birthYear = input.age.map { input.calendar.component(.year, from: currentTime.now()) - $0 }

1	//  _______  __   __  ___   ___      ___      _______  __    _  _______
2	// |       ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |   _   ||  |  | ||       |

8	//
9	// Half-Life SleepNeedRule
10	//
11	
12	import Foundation
13	
14	/// Chooses the nightly sleep recommended for the user's age.
15	///
16	/// The age is the current year, in the given calendar, minus the birth year, so it can be a year ahead of the user's
17	/// real age. With no birth year, it's the adult range. It's stateless and reads no clock. The Onboarding article lists
18	/// its requirements, SLEEPNEED-1 to SLEEPNEED-3.
19	nonisolated struct SleepNeedRule {
20	    /// The first age that gets the adult range.
21	    static let adultAge = 18
22	    /// The first age that gets the older adult range.
23	    static let olderAdultAge = 65
24	
25	    /// Returns the recommended sleep for someone born in `birthYear`.
26	    ///
27	    /// - Parameters:
28	    ///   - birthYear: The year the user's age implies, or `nil` if they didn't give one.
29	    ///   - now: The current time.
30	    ///   - calendar: The calendar whose year the age is counted in.
31	    /// - Returns: The range for the user's age.
32	    func recommendedSleep(birthYear: Int?, now: Date, calendar: Calendar) -> RecommendedSleep {
33	        guard let birthYear else { return .adult }
34	        let age = calendar.component(.year, from: now) - birthYear
35	        if age < Self.adultAge {
36	            return .teen
37	        }
38	        return age < Self.olderAdultAge ? .adult : .olderAdult
39	    }
40	}
```

Half-Life/Documentation.docc/TodayScreen.md:454:| UI-2 | The Today screen passes the system accessibility audit (constitution Article VI.4), twice. At launch, contrast is ignored only for elements under the tab bar or in its fade, 44 pt above the log button, and for issues with no element. Scrolled to the end, contrast is ignored only for elements that reach above where the content started at rest, and only if the first audit checked them in full. Half-Life/Documentation.docc/TodayScreen.md:463:| UI-9 | The history card passes the accessibility audit, with and without a deletion waiting for confirmation. The test first scrolls until the card's total is above the log button, so the whole card is audited clear of the tab bar. It then audits with `auditAccessibilityAboveTheTabBar()`, like every screen in the root tab bar (<doc:Architecture>), so only the cards still under the bar are exempt from the contrast check. |
Half-Life/Documentation.docc/Architecture.md:229:A robot conforms to `Robot` (`Half-LifeUITests/Robots/Robot.swift`) and names its screen's identifier. It owns a private field for each element it controls. Its API is made of semantic commands and verifications, and commands never return another robot. Every robot gets `auditAccessibility()` and `screenshot()` from the protocol, and is listed in `Robots.all` so resolution failures can name the screen that's showing.
Half-Life/Documentation.docc/Architecture.md:264:    try log.auditAccessibility()
Half-Life/Documentation.docc/Architecture.md:293:| `SiriShortcutsRobot` | ``SiriShortcutsView``, onboarding's "Use Siri and Shortcuts" step | `SiriShortcutsViewAccessibilityID` (`screen`, `content`, `logPhrase`, `levelPhrase`, `sleepPhrase`, `askPhrase`, `shortcutsLink`, `continueButton`). Its audit, `auditAccessibilityExcusingTheShortcutsButton()`, excuses only clipped text inside Apple's `ShortcutsLink`, an exception the owner approved on 2026-09-13 (<doc:Onboarding>). |
Half-Life/Documentation.docc/Architecture.md:309:Screens in the root tab bar run `auditAccessibilityAboveTheTabBar()` instead of `auditAccessibility()`. The Today screen scrolls under the tab bar, so at launch its lowest cards sit behind the bar, and the audit reads their contrast against the bar. The helper audits twice. At launch, it ignores contrast issues only for elements that reach under the bar or into its fade, 44 pt above the log button, and for issues the audit can't tie to an element, never for the log button itself, and fails on every other issue. Then it swipes up until the screen's first text stops moving, and audits again, so every card that was behind the bar is checked in full. At the end of a screen a little taller than the phone, the top of the content, such as a pushed Settings screen's title, sits under the navigation bar and in its fade. So the second audit ignores a contrast issue only for an element that reaches above where the content started at rest, and only if the first audit checked that element in full, because at rest it sat wholly above the bottom fade. Before each audit, it waits for a still screen: it takes screenshots until three in a row are identical, or 30 have been taken. After a launch or a scroll, the bar's glass and the scroll edge effect keep animating after the content stops, and audits taken then failed contrast on text that passes once the screen is still. Waiting for that condition, rather than for a fixed time, keeps to constitution Article II.9. The owner approved the exception and the second audit on 2026-09-12 (<doc:OneTapLog>), and chose the wait the same day, after three audits failed intermittently. On 2026-09-13, the wait grew from two identical screenshots of 20 to three of 30, after a long Today screen's second audit failed while it still moved, and the owner approved the top of the second audit (<doc:Settings>).

**15:31:18 · Tool result**

```
import Foundation

/// The time of day the user wants to be asleep by.
///
/// It's a time of day, not a date: the decay card finds the next time it comes round, in the user's calendar. It's
/// always a real time of day, from 0:00 to 23:59. See the Today Screen article, BED-1 and BED-2.
nonisolated struct Bedtime: Sendable, Equatable {
    /// 10:30pm, the prototype's example. It applies until the user sets their own bedtime.
    static let standard = Bedtime(validHour: 22, minute: 30)

    /// The hour, from 0 to 23.
    let hour: Int
    /// The minute, from 0 to 59.
    let minute: Int

    /// Creates a bedtime, or returns `nil` if `hour` and `minute` aren't a time of day.
    ///
    /// - Parameters:
    ///   - hour: The hour, from 0 to 23.
    ///   - minute: The minute, from 0 to 59.
    init?(hour: Int, minute: Int) {
        guard (0..<24).contains(hour), (0..<60).contains(minute) else { return nil }
        self.hour = hour
        self.minute = minute
    }

    private init(validHour hour: Int, minute: Int) {
        self.hour = hour
        self.minute = minute
    }
}

extension Bedtime {
    /// Returns the first time the bedtime comes round at or after `date`: tonight's, or tomorrow's once tonight's has
    /// passed. See the Today Screen article, BED-3.
    ///
    /// - Parameters:
    ///   - date: The moment to look from, usually the current time.
    ///   - calendar: The calendar, and so the time zone, the bedtime is a time of day in.
    /// - Returns: The next bedtime, or `nil` if the calendar can't find one.
```

- `message`: ``half-life-ce is replacing the age wheel with a date-of-birth picker (onboarding About you + Settings About you); are you editing any of these files?     /// UI-ONB-3: every step passes the system accessibility audit (constitution Article VI.4).
    @MainActor
    func testEveryStepPassesTheAccessibilityAudit() throws {
        let app = XCUIApplication()
        app.launchAtOnboarding()

        let welcome = try app.resolve(WelcomeRobot.self)
        try welcome.auditAccessibility()
        welcome.getStarted()
        let aboutYou = try app.resolve(AboutYouRobot.self)
        try aboutYou.auditAccessibility()
        aboutYou.continueToNextStep()
        let factors = try app.resolve(HalfLifeFactorsRobot.self)
        factors.choosePregnancy(trimester: .second)
        factors.verifyPregnancy(trimester: .second)
        try factors.auditAccessibility()
        factors.continueToNextStep()
        let bedtime = try app.resolve(BedtimeRobot.self)
----

    /// UI-SET-2: a name saved on the About you screen greets the user on the Today screen.
    @MainActor
    func testANameSavedInSettingsGreetsTheUserOnToday() throws {
        let (app, settings) = try launchIntoSettings()
        settings.open(.aboutYou)
        try app.resolve(AboutYouSettingsRobot.self).enterName("Alex")

        try app.resolve(AppRobot.self).openToday()
        try app.resolve(TodayRobot.self).verifyGreeting(names: "Alex")
    }

    /// UI-SET-3: choosing a factor changes the starting half-life: estrogen gives 8.3 hours.

    }

/// An option that's chosen or not, in the style of onboarding's factor options: a check circle, a title, and a line
/// about it. A chosen option has the selected trait, so VoiceOver doesn't depend on its fill (Article VI.3).
///
/// Settings uses it where iOS would use a switch, because the accessibility audit failed the system switch's rows.
@MainActor
struct SettingsOption: View {
    /// The option's title.
    let title: Text
    /// A line about the option, if it has one.
    let detail: Text?
    /// Whether the option is chosen.
    let isSelected: Bool
    /// The option's accessibility identifier.
    let identifier: String
    /// What tapping the option does.
    let action: () -> Void

    /// The option's row.
    var body: some View {
        Button(action: action) {
            HStack(alignment: .top, spacing: Spacing.itemGap) {
                Image(systemName: isSelected ? "checkmark.circle.fill" : "circle")
                    .font(.title3)
                    .foregroundStyle(isSelected ? Color.accentOnEmphasis : Color.controlBorder)
                    .accessibilityHidden(true)
                VStack(alignment: .leading) {
                    title
                        .font(.callout.weight(.semibold))
                        .foregroundStyle(isSelected ? Color.textOnEmphasis : Color.textPrimary)
                    // The line is in textPrimary, not onboarding's textSecondary: the accessibility audit failed
                    // textSecondary footnotes on these cards, at about 5.9:1 by the tokens. The owner chose this on
                    // 2026-09-13.
                    if let detail {
                        detail
                            .font(.footnote)
                            .foregroundStyle(isSelected ? Color.textOnEmphasisSecondary : Color.textPrimary)
```

//  _______  __   __  ___   ___      ___      _______  __    _  _______
// |       ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |   _   ||  |  | ||       |
// |   _   ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |  |_|  ||   |_| ||   _   |
// |  | |  ||  |_|  ||   | |   |    |   |    |       ||       ||  | |  |
// |  |_|  ||       ||   | |   |___ |   |___ |       ||  _    ||  |_|  |
// |      | |       ||   | |       ||       ||   _   || | |   ||      |
// |____||_||_______||___| |_______||_______||__| |__||_|  |__||____||_|
//
// Half-LifeTests DateOfBirthTests
//

import Foundation
import Testing

@testable import Half_Life

/// Checks the date of birth against DOB-1 to DOB-3 in the Onboarding article.
struct DateOfBirthTests {

    static let utc: Calendar = {
        var calendar = Calendar(identifier: .gregorian)
        calendar.timeZone = .gmt
        return calendar
    }()

    static let newYork: Calendar = {
        var calendar = Calendar(identifier: .gregorian)
        calendar.timeZone = TimeZone(identifier: "America/New_York") ?? .gmt
        return calendar
    }()

    static func utcDate(_ year: Int, _ month: Int, _ day: Int, hour: Int = 0, minute: Int = 0) -> Date? {
        utc.date(from: DateComponents(year: year, month: month, day: day, hour: hour, minute: minute))
    }

    // MARK: - DOB-1: a date of birth is a real day in the Gregorian calendar

    @Test func aRealDayMakesADateOfBirth() throws {
        let dateOfBirth = try #require(DateOfBirth(year: 1990, month: 3, day: 3))

        #expect(dateOfBirth.year == 1990)
        #expect(dateOfBirth.month == 3)
        #expect(dateOfBirth.day == 3)
        #expect(DateOfBirth(year: 2024, month: 2, day: 29) != nil)
    }

    @Test func aDayThatDoesntExistMakesNone() {
        #expect(DateOfBirth(year: 2025, month: 2, day: 29) == nil)
        #expect(DateOfBirth(year: 1990, month: 4, day: 31) == nil)
        #expect(DateOfBirth(year: 1990, month: 13, day: 1) == nil)
        #expect(DateOfBirth(year: 1990, month: 1, day: 0) == nil)
    }

    // MARK: - DOB-2: it's the day a moment falls on in the calendar's time zone, and back

    @Test func takesTheDayAMomentFallsOnInTheCalendarsTimeZone() throws {
        // 2:00am UTC on 4 March 1990 is still 3 March in New York.
        let moment = try #require(Self.utcDate(1990, 3, 4, hour: 2))

        #expect(DateOfBirth(moment, in: Self.utc) == DateOfBirth(year: 1990, month: 3, day: 4))
        #expect(DateOfBirth(moment, in: Self.newYork) == DateOfBirth(year: 1990, month: 3, day: 3))
    }

    @Test func isTheStartOfItsDayInTheCalendarsTimeZone() throws {
        let dateOfBirth = try #require(DateOfBirth(year: 1990, month: 3, day: 3))

        #expect(dateOfBirth.date(in: Self.utc) == Self.utcDate(1990, 3, 3))
        #expect(dateOfBirth.date(in: Self.newYork) == Self.utcDate(1990, 3, 3, hour: 5))
    }

    @Test func countsTheDayInTheGregorianCalendarWhateverTheUsersCalendar() throws {
        var buddhist = Calendar(identifier: .buddhist)
        buddhist.timeZone = .gmt
        let moment = try #require(Self.utcDate(1990, 3, 3, hour: 12))

        let dateOfBirth = try #require(DateOfBirth(moment, in: buddhist))

        #expect(dateOfBirth == DateOfBirth(year: 1990, month: 3, day: 3))
        #expect(dateOfBirth.date(in: buddhist) == Self.utcDate(1990, 3, 3))
    }

    // MARK: - DOB-3: the age is the whole years to a moment, in the calendar's time zone

    @Test func theAgeTurnsOverOnTheBirthday() throws {
        let dateOfBirth = try #require(DateOfBirth(year: 2008, month: 6, day: 15))
        let dayBefore = try #require(Self.utcDate(2026, 6, 14, hour: 23, minute: 59))
        let birthday = try #require(Self.utcDate(2026, 6, 15))

        #expect(dateOfBirth.age(at: dayBefore, in: Self.utc) == 17)
        #expect(dateOfBirth.age(at: birthday, in: Self.utc) == 18)
    }

    @Test func theAgeFollowsTheCalendarsTimeZone() throws {
        // 2:00am UTC on 15 June 2026 is still 14 June in New York.
        let dateOfBirth = try #require(DateOfBirth(year: 2008, month: 6, day: 15))
        let moment = try #require(Self.utcDate(2026, 6, 15, hour: 2))

        #expect(dateOfBirth.age(at: moment, in: Self.utc) == 18)
        #expect(dateOfBirth.age(at: moment, in: Self.newYork) == 17)
    }
}

```

8	//
9	// Half-LifeTests FakeUserProfileRepository
10	//
11	
12	import Foundation
13	
14	@testable import Half_Life
15	
16	/// An in-memory user profile repository for use case and reducer tests.
17	///
18	/// It streams the profiles and recommended sleep it was given, then finishes, and records every save. It's an actor,
19	/// like the live repositories.
20	actor FakeUserProfileRepository: UserProfileRepository {
21	    /// One call to `saveAboutYou(name:birthYear:)`.
22	    struct SavedAboutYou: Equatable, Sendable {
23	        let name: String?
24	        let birthYear: Int?
25	    }
26	
27	    /// The profiles that `profile()` streams, in order.
28	    let profiles: [UserProfile]
29	    /// The ranges that `recommendedSleep(in:)` streams, in order.
30	    let recommendedSleeps: [RecommendedSleep]
31	    /// The calendar `recommendedSleep(in:)` expects. Given any other, it streams nothing.
32	    let sleepCalendar: Calendar?
33	    /// The error every save throws, if any.
34	    let error: (any Error)?
35	
36	    private(set) var savedAboutYou: [SavedAboutYou] = []
37	    private(set) var savedFactors: [Set<HalfLifeFactor>] = []
38	    private(set) var savedBedtimes: [Bedtime] = []
39	    private(set) var completeCount = 0
40

//  _______  __   __  ___   ___      ___      _______  __    _  _______
// |       ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |   _   ||  |  | ||       |
// |   _   ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |  |_|  ||   |_| ||   _   |
// |  | |  ||  |_|  ||   | |   |    |   |    |       ||       ||  | |  |
// |  |_|  ||       ||   | |   |___ |   |___ |       ||  _    ||  |_|  |
// |      | |       ||   | |       ||       ||   _   || | |   ||      |
// |____||_||_______||___| |_______||_______||__| |__||_|  |__||____||_|
//
// Half-LifeTests SleepNeedRuleTests
//

import Foundation
import Testing

@testable import Half_Life

struct SleepNeedRuleTests {

    let rule = SleepNeedRule()

    static let utc: Calendar = {
        var calendar = Calendar(identifier: .gregorian)
        calendar.timeZone = .gmt
        return calendar
    }()

    /// Midsummer 2026, in UTC.
    static let now: Date = {
        let components = DateComponents(year: 2026, month: 6, day: 15, hour: 12)
        return utc.date(from: components) ?? .distantPast
    }()

    func sleep(
        bornOn year: Int, _ month: Int, _ day: Int, now: Date = now, calendar: Calendar = utc
    ) throws -> RecommendedSleep {
        let dateOfBirth = try #require(DateOfBirth(year: year, month: month, day: day))
        return rule.recommendedSleep(dateOfBirth: dateOfBirth, now: now, calendar: calendar)
    }

    // MARK: - SLEEPNEED-1: each age range gets its recommended sleep

    @Test func teenagersNeedEightToTenHours() throws {
        #expect(try sleep(bornOn: 2013, 6, 15) == .teen)
        #expect(try sleep(bornOn: 2008, 6, 16) == .teen)
        #expect(RecommendedSleep.teen == RecommendedSleep(minimumHours: 8, maximumHours: 10))
    }

    @Test func adultsNeedSevenToNineHours() throws {
        #expect(try sleep(bornOn: 2008, 6, 15) == .adult)
        #expect(try sleep(bornOn: 1961, 6, 16) == .adult)
        #expect(RecommendedSleep.adult == RecommendedSleep(minimumHours: 7, maximumHours: 9))
    }

    @Test func olderAdultsNeedSevenToEightHours() throws {
        #expect(try sleep(bornOn: 1961, 6, 15) == .olderAdult)
        #expect(try sleep(bornOn: 1930, 1, 1) == .olderAdult)
        #expect(RecommendedSleep.olderAdult == RecommendedSleep(minimumHours: 7, maximumHours: 8))
    }

    @Test func aRangeIsInSeconds() {
        #expect(RecommendedSleep.adult.minimumSeconds == 25_200)
        #expect(RecommendedSleep.adult.maximumSeconds == 32_400)
    }

    // MARK: - SLEEPNEED-2: with no date of birth, it's the adult range

    @Test func withNoDateOfBirthItsTheAdultRange() {
        #expect(rule.recommendedSleep(dateOfBirth: nil, now: Self.now, calendar: Self.utc) == .adult)
    }

    // MARK: - SLEEPNEED-3: the age is the whole years since the date of birth, in the calendar's time zone

    @Test func theAgeFollowsTheCalendarsTimeZone() throws {
        // 2:00am UTC on 15 June 2026 is still 14 June in New York, the day before an 18th birthday.
        let morning = try #require(Self.utc.date(from: DateComponents(year: 2026, month: 6, day: 15, hour: 2)))
        var newYork = Calendar(identifier: .gregorian)
        newYork.timeZone = try #require(TimeZone(identifier: "America/New_York"))

        #expect(try sleep(bornOn: 2008, 6, 15, now: morning, calendar: Self.utc) == .adult)
        #expect(try sleep(bornOn: 2008, 6, 15, now: morning, calendar: newYork) == .teen)
    }
}

```

            Self.saveAboutYou(repository), SaveAboutYouUseCase.Input(name: "Alex", age: 30, calendar: Self.utc))

        #expect(await repository.savedAboutYou == [.init(name: "Alex", birthYear: 1996)])

    @Test func aVersionOneFilesBirthYearReadsAsNoDateOfBirth() async throws {
        let url = Self.temporaryFile()
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(
            at: url.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let versionOne = """
            {"version": 1, "name": "Alex", "birthYear": 1990, "halfLifeFactors": ["smokes"], "bedtimeHour": 23,
            "bedtimeMinute": 45, "halfLifeSeconds": 37620, "hasCompletedOnboarding": true}
            """
        try Data(versionOne.utf8).write(to: url)

        let profile = try await FileProfileDataSource(fileURL: url).storedProfile()

        #expect(
            profile
                == UserProfile(
                    name: "Alex", halfLifeFactors: [.smokes], bedtime: try #require(Bedtime(hour: 23, minute: 45)),
                    halfLife: try #require(CaffeineHalfLife(seconds: 37_620)), hasCompletedOnboarding: true))
    }

    @Test func aDateOfBirthThatIsntARealDayThrows() async throws {
        let url = Self.temporaryFile()
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(
            at: url.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let damaged = """
            {"version": 2, "dateOfBirth": {"year": 1990, "month": 2, "day": 30}, "halfLifeFactors": [],
            "bedtimeHour": 22, "bedtimeMinute": 30, "halfLifeSeconds": 37620, "hasCompletedOnboarding": false}
            """
        try Data(damaged.utf8).write(to: url)

        await #expect(throws: (any Error).self) {
            try await FileProfileDataSource(fileURL: url).storedProfile()
        }
    }

    // MARK: - A damaged file throws rather than reading as nothing

//  _______  __   __  ___   ___      ___      _______  __    _  _______

//
// Half-LifeTests AboutYouFeatureTests
//

import ComposableArchitecture
import Foundation
import Testing

@testable import Half_Life

/// Checks the About you step (ONB-5 in the Onboarding article) and its date-of-birth picker (ONB-10).
@MainActor
struct AboutYouFeatureTests {

    static let utc: Calendar = {
        var calendar = Calendar(identifier: .gregorian)
        calendar.timeZone = .gmt
        return calendar
    }()

    /// Midsummer 2026, in UTC.
    static let now = day(2026, 6, 15, hour: 15)
    static let timeOfDay = TimeOfDay(date: now, period: .afternoon)
    static let picker = BirthDatePicker(today: now, calendar: utc)

    /// A moment on a day in UTC.
    static func day(_ year: Int, _ month: Int, _ day: Int, hour: Int = 0) -> Date {
        utc.date(from: DateComponents(year: year, month: month, day: day, hour: hour)) ?? .distantPast
    }

    // MARK: - The step reads the saved profile and the picker's dates

    @Test func taskReducesTheSavedProfileIntoState() async {
        let profile = UserProfile(name: "Alex", dateOfBirth: DateOfBirth(year: 1990, month: 3, day: 3))
        let store = TestStore(initialState: AboutYouFeature.State()) {
            AboutYouFeature()
        } withDependencies: {
            $0.calendar = Self.utc
            $0.observeUserProfile = ObserveUserProfileUseCase(
                repository: FakeUserProfileRepository(profiles: [profile]))
            $0.observeTimeOfDay = ObserveTimeOfDayUseCase(currentTime: SilentCurrentTimeRepository())
        }

        await store.send(.task)
        await store.receive(\.profileUpdated) {
            $0.savedProfile = profile
        }
        await store.finish()
    }

    @Test func taskReducesThePickersDatesIntoState() async {
        let store = TestStore(initialState: AboutYouFeature.State()) {
            AboutYouFeature()
        } withDependencies: {
            $0.calendar = Self.utc
            $0.observeUserProfile = ObserveUserProfileUseCase(repository: FakeUserProfileRepository(profiles: []))
            $0.observeTimeOfDay = ObserveTimeOfDayUseCase(currentTime: FakeCurrentTimeRepository(date: Self.now))
        }

        await store.send(.task)
        await store.receive(\.timeOfDayUpdated) {
            $0.birthDatePicker = Self.picker
        }
        await store.finish()
    }

    // MARK: - ONB-10: the picker offers ages 13 to 100, and suggests a 30-year-old's date of birth

    @Test func thePickerOffersThirteenToOneHundredYearsAgoFromTheStartOfToday() throws {
        let picker = try #require(Self.picker)

        #expect(picker.dates == Self.day(1926, 6, 15)...Self.day(2013, 6, 15))
        #expect(picker.suggestedDate == Self.day(1996, 6, 15))
        #expect(BirthDatePicker.ages == 13...100)
    }

    // MARK: - The fields fill in from the saved profile once, unless the user has changed them

    @Test func theFieldsFillInOnceTheProfileAndThePickerAreKnown() async {
        let store = TestStore(initialState: AboutYouFeature.State()) {
            AboutYouFeature()
        } withDependencies: {
            $0.calendar = Self.utc
        }
        let alex = UserProfile(name: "Alex", dateOfBirth: DateOfBirth(year: 1990, month: 3, day: 3))
        let sam = UserProfile(name: "Sam", dateOfBirth: DateOfBirth(year: 1980, month: 1, day: 1))

        await store.send(.profileUpdated(alex)) {
            $0.savedProfile = alex
        }
        await store.send(.timeOfDayUpdated(Self.timeOfDay)) {
            $0.birthDatePicker = Self.picker
            $0.name = "Alex"
            $0.birthDate = Self.day(1990, 3, 3)
            $0.hasFilledFields = true
        }
        await store.send(.profileUpdated(sam)) {
            $0.savedProfile = sam
        }
    }

    @Test func aSavedDateOfBirthOutsideThePickerIsLeftEmpty() async {
        let store = TestStore(initialState: AboutYouFeature.State()) {
            AboutYouFeature()
        } withDependencies: {
            $0.calendar = Self.utc
        }
        let child = UserProfile(dateOfBirth: DateOfBirth(year: 2020, month: 1, day: 1))

        await store.send(.timeOfDayUpdated(Self.timeOfDay)) {
            $0.birthDatePicker = Self.picker
        }
        await store.send(.profileUpdated(child)) {
            $0.savedProfile = child
            $0.hasFilledFields = true
        }
    }

    @Test func typingBeforeTheProfileArrivesKeepsWhatWasTyped() async {
        let store = TestStore(initialState: AboutYouFeature.State()) {
            AboutYouFeature()
        } withDependencies: {
            $0.calendar = Self.utc
        }
        let alex = UserProfile(name: "Alex", dateOfBirth: DateOfBirth(year: 1990, month: 3, day: 3))

        await store.send(.binding(.set(\.name, "Sam"))) {
            $0.name = "Sam"
            $0.hasFilledFields = true
        }
        await store.send(.timeOfDayUpdated(Self.timeOfDay)) {
            $0.birthDatePicker = Self.picker
        }
        await store.send(.profileUpdated(alex)) {
            $0.savedProfile = alex
        }
    }

    @Test func choosingADateKeepsIt() async {
        let store = TestStore(initialState: AboutYouFeature.State()) {
            AboutYouFeature()
        }

        await store.send(.birthDateChosen(Self.day(1984, 4, 2))) {
            $0.birthDate = Self.day(1984, 4, 2)
            $0.hasFilledFields = true
        }
    }

    @Test func preferringNotToSayClearsTheDate() async {
        let store = TestStore(initialState: AboutYouFeature.State(birthDate: Self.day(1984, 4, 2))) {
            AboutYouFeature()
        }

        await store.send(.preferNotToSayTapped) {
            $0.birthDate = nil
            $0.hasFilledFields = true
        }
    }

    // MARK: - ONB-5: Continue saves the name and date of birth, then moves on

    @Test func continueSavesTheNameAndDateOfBirthThenContinues() async {
        let repository = FakeUserProfileRepository()
        let store = TestStore(initialState: AboutYouFeature.State(name: "Alex", birthDate: Self.day(1996, 3, 3))) {
            AboutYouFeature()
        } withDependencies: {
            $0.calendar = Self.utc
            $0.saveAboutYou = SaveAboutYouUseCase(profile: repository)
        }

        await store.send(.continueTapped) {
            $0.isSaving = true
        }
        await store.receive(\.saveFinished) {
            $0.isSaving = false
        }
        await store.receive(\.delegate.continued)

        #expect(
            await repository.savedAboutYou == [
                .init(name: "Alex", dateOfBirth: DateOfBirth(year: 1996, month: 3, day: 3))
            ])
    }

    @Test func emptyFieldsSaveNothing() async {
        let repository = FakeUserProfileRepository()
        let store = TestStore(initialState: AboutYouFeature.State()) {
            AboutYouFeature()
        } withDependencies: {
            $0.calendar = Self.utc
            $0.saveAboutYou = SaveAboutYouUseCase(profile: repository)
        }

        await store.send(.continueTapped) {
            $0.isSaving = true
        }
        await store.receive(\.saveFinished) {
            $0.isSaving = false
        }
        await store.receive(\.delegate.continued)

        #expect(await repository.savedAboutYou == [.init(name: nil, dateOfBirth: nil)])
    }

    @Test func aFailedSaveStillContinues() async {
        let store = TestStore(initialState: AboutYouFeature.State(name: "Alex")) {
            AboutYouFeature()
        } withDependencies: {
            $0.calendar = Self.utc
            $0.saveAboutYou = SaveAboutYouUseCase(profile: FakeUserProfileRepository(error: FakeDataSourceError()))
        }

        await store.send(.continueTapped) {
            $0.isSaving = true
        }
        await store.receive(\.saveFinished) {
            $0.isSaving = false
        }
        await store.receive(\.delegate.continued)
    }
}

```

// Half-LifeTests ProfileSettingsFeatureTests
//

import ComposableArchitecture
import Foundation
import Testing

@testable import Half_Life

/// Checks Settings' profile sections, the onboarding answers, against SETPROF-1 to SETPROF-7 in the Settings article.
@MainActor
struct ProfileSettingsFeatureTests {

    struct SaveFailed: Error {}

    static let utc: Calendar = {
        var calendar = Calendar(identifier: .gregorian)
        calendar.timeZone = .gmt
        return calendar
    }()

    /// Midsummer 2026, in UTC.
    static let now = day(2026, 6, 15, hour: 15)
    static let timeOfDay = TimeOfDay(date: now, period: .afternoon)
    static let picker = BirthDatePicker(today: now, calendar: utc)
    static let elevenPM = Bedtime(hour: 23, minute: 0) ?? .standard
    static let midnight = Bedtime(hour: 0, minute: 0) ?? .standard

    /// A moment on a day in UTC.
    static func day(_ year: Int, _ month: Int, _ day: Int, hour: Int = 0) -> Date {
        utc.date(from: DateComponents(year: year, month: month, day: day, hour: hour)) ?? .distantPast
    }

    static func store(
        _ state: ProfileSettingsFeature.State, repository: FakeUserProfileRepository = FakeUserProfileRepository()
    ) -> TestStoreOf<ProfileSettingsFeature> {
        TestStore(initialState: state) {
            ProfileSettingsFeature()
        } withDependencies: {
            $0.calendar = Self.utc
            $0.saveAboutYou = SaveAboutYouUseCase(profile: repository)
            $0.saveBedtime = SaveBedtimeUseCase(repository: repository)
            $0.saveHalfLifeFactors = SaveHalfLifeFactorsUseCase(repository: repository)
        }
    }

    // MARK: - SETPROF-1: the sections show the saved profile, refilled from every profile the repository publishes

    @Test func taskReducesTheSavedProfileIntoState() async {
        let halfLife = HalfLifePriorRule().halfLife(for: [.smokes])
        let profile = UserProfile(
            name: "Alex", dateOfBirth: DateOfBirth(year: 1990, month: 3, day: 3), halfLifeFactors: [.smokes],
            bedtime: Self.elevenPM, halfLife: halfLife)
        let store = TestStore(initialState: ProfileSettingsFeature.State()) {
            ProfileSettingsFeature()
        } withDependencies: {
            $0.calendar = Self.utc
            $0.observeUserProfile = ObserveUserProfileUseCase(
                repository: FakeUserProfileRepository(profiles: [profile]))
            $0.observeTimeOfDay = ObserveTimeOfDayUseCase(currentTime: SilentCurrentTimeRepository())
        }

        await store.send(.task)
        await store.receive(\.profileUpdated) {
            $0.savedProfile = profile
            $0.name = "Alex"
            $0.factors = [.smokes]
            $0.bedtime = Self.elevenPM
        }
        await store.finish()
    }

    @Test func taskReducesThePickersDatesIntoState() async {
        let store = TestStore(initialState: ProfileSettingsFeature.State()) {
            ProfileSettingsFeature()
        } withDependencies: {
            $0.calendar = Self.utc
            $0.observeUserProfile = ObserveUserProfileUseCase(repository: FakeUserProfileRepository(profiles: []))
            $0.observeTimeOfDay = ObserveTimeOfDayUseCase(currentTime: FakeCurrentTimeRepository(date: Self.now))
        }

        await store.send(.task)
        await store.receive(\.timeOfDayUpdated) {
            $0.birthDatePicker = Self.picker
        }
        await store.finish()
    }

    @Test func theDateOfBirthFillsInOnceThePickerIsKnownAndEachProfileRefillsTheFields() async {
        let store = Self.store(ProfileSettingsFeature.State())
        let alex = UserProfile(name: "Alex", dateOfBirth: DateOfBirth(year: 1990, month: 3, day: 3))
        let sam = UserProfile(
            name: "Sam", dateOfBirth: DateOfBirth(year: 1980, month: 1, day: 1), bedtime: Self.elevenPM)

        await store.send(.profileUpdated(alex)) {
            $0.savedProfile = alex
            $0.name = "Alex"
        }
        await store.send(.timeOfDayUpdated(Self.timeOfDay)) {
            $0.birthDatePicker = Self.picker
            $0.birthDate = Self.day(1990, 3, 3)
        }
        await store.send(.profileUpdated(sam)) {
            $0.savedProfile = sam
            $0.name = "Sam"
            $0.birthDate = Self.day(1980, 1, 1)
            $0.bedtime = Self.elevenPM
        }
    }

    @Test func aSavedDateOfBirthOutsideThePickerIsLeftEmpty() async {
        let store = Self.store(
            ProfileSettingsFeature.State(birthDate: Self.day(1996, 3, 3), birthDatePicker: Self.picker))
        let child = UserProfile(dateOfBirth: DateOfBirth(year: 2020, month: 1, day: 1))

        await store.send(.profileUpdated(child)) {
            $0.savedProfile = child
            $0.birthDate = nil
        }
    }

    // MARK: - SETPROF-2: the name saves when it's committed, and a name being typed isn't replaced

    @Test func aNameBeingTypedIsntReplacedByAPublishedProfile() async {
        let store = Self.store(ProfileSettingsFeature.State())

        await store.send(.binding(.set(\.name, "Sa"))) {
            $0.name = "Sa"
            $0.isEditingName = true
        }
        await store.send(.profileUpdated(UserProfile(name: "Alex"))) {
            $0.savedProfile = UserProfile(name: "Alex")
        }
    }

    @Test func committingTheNameSavesItWithTheDateOfBirth() async {
        let repository = FakeUserProfileRepository()
        let store = Self.store(
            ProfileSettingsFeature.State(name: " Sam ", birthDate: Self.day(1990, 3, 3), isEditingName: true),
            repository: repository)

        await store.send(.nameCommitted) {
            $0.isEditingName = false
            $0.savesInFlight = 1
        }
        await store.receive(\.saveFinished) {
            $0.savesInFlight = 0
        }

        #expect(
            await repository.savedAboutYou == [
                .init(name: "Sam", dateOfBirth: DateOfBirth(year: 1990, month: 3, day: 3))
            ])
    }

    /// The name field wraps, so Return types a newline instead of submitting. The newline commits the name instead.
    @Test func typingANewlineCommitsTheNameWithoutIt() async {
        let repository = FakeUserProfileRepository()
        let store = Self.store(ProfileSettingsFeature.State(name: "Sam"), repository: repository)

        await store.send(.binding(.set(\.name, "Sam\n"))) {
            $0.name = "Sam"
            $0.savesInFlight = 1
        }
        await store.receive(\.saveFinished) {
            $0.savesInFlight = 0
        }

        #expect(await repository.savedAboutYou == [.init(name: "Sam", dateOfBirth: nil)])
    }

    @Test func committingANameThatWasntChangedSavesNothing() async {
        let repository = FakeUserProfileRepository()
        let store = Self.store(ProfileSettingsFeature.State(name: "Alex"), repository: repository)

        await store.send(.nameCommitted)

        #expect(await repository.savedAboutYou.isEmpty)
    }

        }

        }

        #expect(
            await repository.savedAboutYou == [
                .init(name: "Alex", dateOfBirth: DateOfBirth(year: 1986, month: 3, day: 3))
            ])
    }

    @Test func preferringNotToSayClearsTheDateAndSavesIt() async {
        let repository = FakeUserProfileRepository()
        let store = Self.store(
            ProfileSettingsFeature.State(name: "Alex", birthDate: Self.day(1986, 3, 3)), repository: repository)

        await store.send(.preferNotToSayTapped) {

            $0.savesInFlight = 1
        }
        await store.receive(\.saveFinished) {
            $0.savesInFlight = 0
        }

    @Test func preferringNotToSayWithNoDateSavesNothing() async {

        let store = Self.store(ProfileSettingsFeature.State(name: "Alex"), repository: repository)

        #expect(await repository.savedAboutYou.isEmpty)

    @Test func aFailedSavePutsTheFieldsBackToWhatsSaved() async {

            repository: FakeUserProfileRepository(error: SaveFailed()))

//  _______  __   __  ___   ___      ___      _______  __    _  _______
// |       ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |   _   ||  |  | ||       |
// |   _   ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |  |_|  ||   |_| ||   _   |
// |  | |  ||  |_|  ||   | |   |    |   |    |       ||       ||  | |  |
// |  |_|  ||       ||   | |   |___ |   |___ |       ||  _    ||  |_|  |
// |      | |       ||   | |       ||       ||   _   || | |   ||      |
// |____||_||_______||___| |_______||_______||__| |__||_|  |__||____||_|
//
// Half-Life DateOfBirth
//

import Foundation

/// The day the user was born: a day in the Gregorian calendar, with no time of day or time zone.
///
/// It's the day itself, not a moment, so it stays the same day when the user changes time zone or calendar. It's
/// always a real day. See the Onboarding article, DOB-1 to DOB-3.
nonisolated struct DateOfBirth: Sendable, Equatable {
    /// The year, in the Gregorian calendar.
    let year: Int
    /// The month, from 1 to 12.
    let month: Int
    /// The day of the month, from 1.
    let day: Int

    /// Creates a date of birth, or returns `nil` if the Gregorian calendar has no such day.
    ///
    /// - Parameters:
    ///   - year: The year, in the Gregorian calendar.
    ///   - month: The month, from 1 to 12.
    ///   - day: The day of the month, from 1.
    init?(year: Int, month: Int, day: Int) {
        guard DateComponents(year: year, month: month, day: day).isValidDate(in: Calendar(identifier: .gregorian))
        else { return nil }
        self.year = year
        self.month = month
        self.day = day
    }
}

extension DateOfBirth {
    /// Creates the date of birth `moment` falls on in `calendar`'s time zone, or returns `nil` if the calendar can't
    /// say which day that is.
    ///
    /// - Parameters:
    ///   - moment: A moment on the day of birth, such as the one a date picker chose.
    ///   - calendar: The user's calendar. Only its time zone counts: the day is always Gregorian.
    init?(_ moment: Date, in calendar: Calendar) {
        let components = Self.gregorian(in: calendar).dateComponents([.year, .month, .day], from: moment)
        guard let year = components.year, let month = components.month, let day = components.day else { return nil }
        self.init(year: year, month: month, day: day)
    }

    /// Returns the start of the day of birth in `calendar`'s time zone, for a date picker to show.
    ///
    /// - Parameter calendar: The user's calendar. Only its time zone counts.
    /// - Returns: The start of the day, or `nil` if the calendar can't find it.
    func date(in calendar: Calendar) -> Date? {
        Self.gregorian(in: calendar).date(from: DateComponents(year: year, month: month, day: day))
    }

    /// Returns the user's age at `moment`: the whole years since the start of the day of birth, in `calendar`'s time
    /// zone. The age goes up at the start of the birthday.
    ///
    /// - Parameters:
    ///   - moment: The moment to count to, usually the current time.
    ///   - calendar: The user's calendar. Only its time zone counts.
    /// - Returns: The age in whole years, or `nil` if the calendar can't count them.
    func age(at moment: Date, in calendar: Calendar) -> Int? {
        guard let birth = date(in: calendar) else { return nil }
        return Self.gregorian(in: calendar).dateComponents([.year], from: birth, to: moment).year
    }

    /// The Gregorian calendar in `calendar`'s time zone.
    private static func gregorian(in calendar: Calendar) -> Calendar {
        var gregorian = Calendar(identifier: .gregorian)
        gregorian.timeZone = calendar.timeZone
        return gregorian
    }
}

//  _______  __   __  ___   ___      ___      _______  __    _  _______
// |       ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |   _   ||  |  | ||       |
// |   _   ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |  |_|  ||   |_| ||   _   |
// |  | |  ||  |_|  ||   | |   |    |   |    |       ||       ||  | |  |
// |  |_|  ||       ||   | |   |___ |   |___ |       ||  _    ||  |_|  |
// |      | |       ||   | |       ||       ||   _   || | |   ||      |
// |____||_||_______||___| |_______||_______||__| |__||_|  |__||____||_|
//
// Half-Life BirthDatePicker
//

import Foundation

/// The dates the date-of-birth picker offers, and the one it shows while "Prefer not to say" is chosen.
///
/// It offers the dates from 100 years ago to 13 years ago, counted back from the start of today in the user's
/// calendar, and shows a 30-year-old's date of birth until the user picks one. Onboarding's About you step and
/// Settings' About you screen share it. See the Onboarding article, ONB-10.
struct BirthDatePicker: Sendable, Equatable {
    /// The ages the picker offers. Younger children aren't the app's audience.
    static let ages = 13...100
    /// The age whose date of birth the picker shows before the user picks one.
    static let suggestedAge = 30

    /// The dates the picker offers, from the oldest age's to the youngest's.
    let dates: ClosedRange<Date>
    /// The date the picker shows while "Prefer not to say" is chosen.
    let suggestedDate: Date

    /// Creates the picker's dates for `today`, or returns `nil` if `calendar` can't count back the years.
    ///
    /// - Parameters:
    ///   - today: The current time.
    ///   - calendar: The user's calendar, whose start of today the years are counted back from.
    init?(today: Date, calendar: Calendar) {
        let startOfToday = calendar.startOfDay(for: today)
        func yearsAgo(_ years: Int) -> Date? {
            calendar.date(byAdding: .year, value: -years, to: startOfToday)
        }
        guard let earliest = yearsAgo(Self.ages.upperBound), let latest = yearsAgo(Self.ages.lowerBound),
            let suggested = yearsAgo(Self.suggestedAge)
        else { return nil }
        dates = earliest...latest
        suggestedDate = suggested
    }
}

12	import Foundation
13	
14	/// Chooses the nightly sleep recommended for the user's age.
15	///
16	/// The age is the current year, in the given calendar, minus the birth year, so it can be a year ahead of the user's
17	/// real age. With no birth year, it's the adult range. It's stateless and reads no clock. The Onboarding article lists
18	/// its requirements, SLEEPNEED-1 to SLEEPNEED-3.
19	nonisolated struct SleepNeedRule {
20	    /// The first age that gets the adult range.
21	    static let adultAge = 18
22	    /// The first age that gets the older adult range.
23	    static let olderAdultAge = 65
24	
25	    /// Returns the recommended sleep for someone born in `birthYear`.
26	    ///
27	    /// - Parameters:
28	    ///   - birthYear: The year the user's age implies, or `nil` if they didn't give one.
29	    ///   - now: The current time.
30	    ///   - calendar: The calendar whose year the age is counted in.
31	    /// - Returns: The range for the user's age.
32	    func recommendedSleep(birthYear: Int?, now: Date, calendar: Calendar) -> RecommendedSleep {
33	        guard let birthYear else { return .adult }
34	        let age = calendar.component(.year, from: now) - birthYear
35	        if age < Self.adultAge {
36	            return .teen
37	        }
38	        return age < Self.olderAdultAge ? .adult : .olderAdult
39	    }
40	}
41

///
/// The name is trimmed, and a blank one is saved as none. The age is saved as the birth year it implies: the

    /// The repository that stores the profile.

/// Saves the name and date of birth the user gave in About you, in onboarding or Settings.

/// The profile as it's written to the file. A version field leaves room to migrate the format later.
///
/// Version 2 replaced version 1's birth year with a date of birth. A version 1 file's birth year isn't read, so its
/// profile has no date of birth until the user gives one (PROFFILE-5).
private struct StoredProfile: Codable {
    var version = 2
    var name: String?
    var dateOfBirth: StoredDateOfBirth?
    var halfLifeFactors: [String]
    var bedtimeHour: Int
    var bedtimeMinute: Int
    var halfLifeSeconds: TimeInterval
    var hasCompletedOnboarding: Bool

    init(_ profile: UserProfile) {
        name = profile.name
        dateOfBirth = profile.dateOfBirth.map(StoredDateOfBirth.init)
```

    /// - Throws: `DecodingError.dataCorrupted` if a value isn't valid, such as an unknown factor or a bedtime that
    ///   isn't a time of day.
    func profile() throws -> UserProfile {
        guard let bedtime = Bedtime(hour: bedtimeHour, minute: bedtimeMinute),
            let halfLife = CaffeineHalfLife(seconds: halfLifeSeconds)
        else {
            throw Self.damaged("The stored bedtime or half-life isn't valid.")
        }
        let factors = try halfLifeFactors.map { code in
            guard let factor = HalfLifeFactor(storedCode: code) else { throw Self.damaged("Unknown factor.") }
            return factor
        }
        return UserProfile(
            name: name, birthYear: birthYear, halfLifeFactors: Set(factors), bedtime: bedtime, halfLife: halfLife,
            hasCompletedOnboarding: hasCompletedOnboarding)
    }

    private static func damaged(_ description: String) -> DecodingError {
        .dataCorrupted(DecodingError.Context(codingPath: [], debugDescription: description))
    }
```

```
    /// - Throws: `DecodingError.dataCorrupted` if a value isn't valid, such as an unknown factor, a bedtime that
    ///   isn't a time of day, or a date of birth that isn't a real day.
    func profile() throws -> UserProfile {
        guard let bedtime = Bedtime(hour: bedtimeHour, minute: bedtimeMinute),
            let halfLife = CaffeineHalfLife(seconds: halfLifeSeconds)
        else {
            throw Self.damaged("The stored bedtime or half-life isn't valid.")
        }
        let factors = try halfLifeFactors.map { code in
            guard let factor = HalfLifeFactor(storedCode: code) else { throw Self.damaged("Unknown factor.") }
            return factor
        }
        let dateOfBirth = try self.dateOfBirth.map { stored in
            guard let dateOfBirth = DateOfBirth(year: stored.year, month: stored.month, day: stored.day) else {
                throw Self.damaged("The stored date of birth isn't a real day.")
            }
            return dateOfBirth
        }
        return UserProfile(
            name: name, dateOfBirth: dateOfBirth, halfLifeFactors: Set(factors), bedtime: bedtime,
            halfLife: halfLife, hasCompletedOnboarding: hasCompletedOnboarding)
    }

    private static func damaged(_ description: String) -> DecodingError {
        .dataCorrupted(DecodingError.Context(codingPath: [], debugDescription: description))
    }
}

/// A date of birth as it's written to the file: the Gregorian year, month, and day.
private struct StoredDateOfBirth: Codable {
    var year: Int
    var month: Int
    var day: Int

    init(_ dateOfBirth: DateOfBirth) {
        year = dateOfBirth.year
        month = dateOfBirth.month
        day = dateOfBirth.day
    }
```

12	import ComposableArchitecture

16	/// Settings' sections for the onboarding answers: About you, Caffeine and your body, and Bedtime.
17	///
18	/// The factors and the starting half-life come only from the profile the repository publishes, and an option saves at
19	/// once (constitution Article I.5). The name, age, and bedtime are the sections' own editing state (Article I.2).
20	/// They're refilled from every published profile, except a name being typed, and except while a save is under way, so
21	/// a wheel being turned never jumps back. The name saves when it's committed, and a newline typed in it commits it,
22	/// because the field wraps and Return types one. The age and the bedtime save as soon as they're chosen. A failed save
23	/// puts the fields back to what was last published. See the Settings article, SETPROF-1 to SETPROF-7.
24	@Reducer nonisolated struct ProfileSettingsFeature {
25	    private static let logger = Logger(for: ProfileSettingsFeature.self)
26	
27	    /// The sections' state.
28	    @ObservableState
29	    struct State: Equatable {
30	        /// The profile the repository last published, or `nil` until it arrives.
31	        var savedProfile: UserProfile?
32	        /// The current year in the user's calendar, or `nil` until the time of day arrives.
33	        var currentYear: Int?
34	        /// The name as the field shows it.
35	        var name = ""
36	        /// The age the picker shows, or `nil` for "Prefer not to say".
37	        var age: Int?
38	        /// The bedtime the picker shows.
39	        var bedtime: Bedtime = .standard
40	        /// The saved factors.
41	        var factors: Set<HalfLifeFactor> = []
42	        /// The saved starting half-life, or `nil` until the profile arrives.
43	        var halfLife: CaffeineHalfLife?
44	        /// Whether the user switched pregnancy on and hasn't chosen a trimester yet.
45	        var isChoosingTrimester = false
46	        /// Whether the name has been typed since it was last filled in or committed. Until it's committed, a published
47	        /// profile doesn't replace it.
48	        var isEditingName = false
49	        /// How many saves of the name, age, or bedtime are under way. While any is, a published profile doesn't move
50	        /// the fields.
51	        var savesInFlight = 0

//  _______  __   __  ___   ___      ___      _______  __    _  _______
// |       ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |   _   ||  |  | ||       |
// |   _   ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |  |_|  ||   |_| ||   _   |
// |  | |  ||  |_|  ||   | |   |    |   |    |       ||       ||  | |  |
// |  |_|  ||       ||   | |   |___ |   |___ |       ||  _    ||  |_|  |
// |      | |       ||   | |       ||       ||   _   || | |   ||      |
// |____||_||_______||___| |_______||_______||__| |__||_|  |__||____||_|
//
// Half-Life BirthDatePicker
//

import Foundation

/// The dates the date-of-birth picker offers, and the one it shows while "Prefer not to say" is chosen.
///
/// It offers the dates from 100 years ago to 13 years ago, counted back from the start of today in the user's
/// calendar, and shows a 30-year-old's date of birth until the user picks one. Onboarding's About you step and
/// Settings' About you screen share it. See the Onboarding article, ONB-10.
struct BirthDatePicker: Sendable, Equatable {
    /// The ages the picker offers. Younger children aren't the app's audience.
    static let ages = 13...100
    /// The age whose date of birth the picker shows before the user picks one.
    static let suggestedAge = 30

    /// The dates the picker offers, from the oldest age's to the youngest's.
    let dates: ClosedRange<Date>
    /// The date the picker shows while "Prefer not to say" is chosen.
    let suggestedDate: Date
    /// The user's calendar, which the years are counted back in.
    let calendar: Calendar

    /// Creates the picker's dates for `today`, or returns `nil` if `calendar` can't count back the years.
    ///
    /// - Parameters:
    ///   - today: The current time.
    ///   - calendar: The user's calendar, whose start of today the years are counted back from.
    init?(today: Date, calendar: Calendar) {
        let startOfToday = calendar.startOfDay(for: today)
        func yearsAgo(_ years: Int) -> Date? {
            calendar.date(byAdding: .year, value: -years, to: startOfToday)
        }
        guard let earliest = yearsAgo(Self.ages.upperBound), let latest = yearsAgo(Self.ages.lowerBound),
            let suggested = yearsAgo(Self.suggestedAge)
        else { return nil }
        dates = earliest...latest
        suggestedDate = suggested
        self.calendar = calendar
    }

    /// Returns the date the picker shows for a saved date of birth: the start of its day in the user's time zone.
    ///
    /// - Parameter dateOfBirth: The saved date of birth, or `nil` if none is saved.
    /// - Returns: The date, or `nil` if none is saved or the picker doesn't offer it.
    func date(of dateOfBirth: DateOfBirth?) -> Date? {
        guard let date = dateOfBirth?.date(in: calendar), dates.contains(date) else { return nil }
        return date
    }
}

```

- `content`:

```
//  _______  __   __  ___   ___      ___      _______  __    _  _______
// |       ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |   _   ||  |  | ||       |
// |   _   ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |  |_|  ||   |_| ||   _   |
// |  | |  ||  |_|  ||   | |   |    |   |    |       ||       ||  | |  |
// |  |_|  ||       ||   | |   |___ |   |___ |       ||  _    ||  |_|  |
// |      | |       ||   | |       ||       ||   _   || | |   ||      |
// |____||_||_______||___| |_______||_______||__| |__||_|  |__||____||_|
//
// Half-Life AboutYouFeature
//

import ComposableArchitecture
import Foundation
import OSLog

/// Onboarding's About you step: the user's first name and date of birth, both optional.
///
/// The fields are the step's own editing state until Continue saves them (constitution Article I.2). They fill in
/// from the saved profile once, when the user returns to onboarding, unless the user has already changed them. The
/// date of birth is a date picker's, and "Prefer not to say" clears it. See the Onboarding article, ONB-5 and ONB-10.
@Reducer nonisolated struct AboutYouFeature {
    private static let logger = Logger(for: AboutYouFeature.self)

    /// The step's state.
    @ObservableState
    struct State: Equatable {
        /// The name as typed.
        var name = ""
        /// The date of birth the picker shows as chosen, or `nil` for "Prefer not to say".
        var birthDate: Date?
        /// The dates the picker offers, or `nil` until the time of day arrives.
        var birthDatePicker: BirthDatePicker?
        /// The profile the repository last published, or `nil` until it arrives.
        var savedProfile: UserProfile?
        /// Whether the fields hold the user's answers, filled in from the saved profile or changed by the user. Once
        /// they do, the saved profile no longer replaces them.
        var hasFilledFields = false
        /// Whether Continue is saving.
        var isSaving = false
    }

    /// What can happen in the step.
    enum Action: BindableAction {
        /// A change to the name.
        case binding(BindingAction<State>)
        /// The step appeared, so it starts observing the profile and the time of day.
        case task
        /// The repository published the profile.
        case profileUpdated(UserProfile)
        /// A new minute arrived, which carries the day the picker's dates are counted back from.
        case timeOfDayUpdated(TimeOfDay)
        /// The user picked a date of birth.
        case birthDateChosen(Date)
        /// The user chose "Prefer not to say".
        case preferNotToSayTapped
        /// The user tapped Continue.
        case continueTapped
        /// The save finished, whether or not it succeeded.
        case saveFinished
        /// What the step tells onboarding.
        case delegate(Delegate)
    }

    /// What the step tells ``OnboardingFeature``.
    @CasePathable
    enum Delegate {
        /// The user continued to the next step.
        case continued
    }

    @Dependency(\.calendar) var calendar
    @Dependency(\.observeUserProfile) var observeUserProfile
    @Dependency(\.observeTimeOfDay) var observeTimeOfDay
    @Dependency(\.saveAboutYou) var saveAboutYou

    /// Keeps the fields, fills them from the saved profile once, and saves them on Continue.
    var body: some ReducerOf<Self> {
        BindingReducer()
        Reduce { state, action in
            switch action {
            case .binding(\.name):
                state.hasFilledFields = true
                return .none
            case .binding:
                return .none
            case .task:
                return .merge(
                    .run { [observeUserProfile] send in
                        for await profile in observeUserProfile.execute(()) {
                            await send(.profileUpdated(profile))
                        }
                    },
                    .run { [observeTimeOfDay, calendar] send in
                        for await timeOfDay in observeTimeOfDay.execute(calendar) {
                            await send(.timeOfDayUpdated(timeOfDay))
                        }
                    }
                )
            case let .profileUpdated(profile):
                state.savedProfile = profile
                Self.fillFields(&state)
                return .none
            case let .timeOfDayUpdated(timeOfDay):
                state.birthDatePicker = BirthDatePicker(today: timeOfDay.date, calendar: calendar)
                Self.fillFields(&state)
                return .none
            case let .birthDateChosen(date):
                state.birthDate = date
                state.hasFilledFields = true
                return .none
            case .preferNotToSayTapped:
                state.birthDate = nil
                state.hasFilledFields = true
                return .none
            case .continueTapped:
                state.isSaving = true
                let input = SaveAboutYouUseCase.Input(
                    name: state.name, dateOfBirth: state.birthDate.flatMap { DateOfBirth($0, in: calendar) })
                return .run { [saveAboutYou] send in
                    do {
                        try await saveAboutYou.execute(input)
                    } catch {
                        // Onboarding never blocks, so the user continues. The answers can be given again later.
                        let error = error as NSError
                        Self.logger.error(
                            "Couldn't save About you: \(error.domain, privacy: .public) \(error.code, privacy: .public)"
                        )
                    }
                    await send(.saveFinished)
                    await send(.delegate(.continued))
                }
            case .saveFinished:
                state.isSaving = false
                return .none
            case .delegate:
                return .none
            }
        }
    }

    /// Fills the fields from the saved profile, once both it and the picker's dates are known, unless they already
    /// hold the user's answers. A saved date of birth the picker doesn't offer is left empty.
    private static func fillFields(_ state: inout State) {
        guard !state.hasFilledFields, let profile = state.savedProfile, let picker = state.birthDatePicker else {
            return
        }
        state.name = profile.name ?? ""
        state.birthDate = picker.date(of: profile.dateOfBirth)
        state.hasFilledFields = true
    }
}

```

/// once (constitution Article I.5). The name, age, and bedtime are the sections' own editing state (Article I.2).
/// They're refilled from every published profile, except a name being typed, and except while a save is under way, so
/// a wheel being turned never jumps back. The name saves when it's committed, and a newline typed in it commits it,
/// because the field wraps and Return types one. The age and the bedtime save as soon as they're chosen. A failed save
/// puts the fields back to what was last published. See the Settings article, SETPROF-1 to SETPROF-7.

/// way, so a picker being changed never jumps back. The name saves when it's committed, and a newline typed in it
/// commits it, because the field wraps and Return types one. The date of birth, "Prefer not to say", and the bedtime
/// save as soon as they're chosen. A failed save puts the fields back to what was last published. See the Settings
/// article, SETPROF-1 to SETPROF-7.

    /// Saves the name as the field shows it, and the date of birth, together.
    private func saveAboutYou(_ state: inout State) -> Effect<Action> {
        state.isEditingName = false
        state.savesInFlight += 1
        let input = SaveAboutYouUseCase.Input(
            name: state.name, dateOfBirth: state.birthDate.flatMap { DateOfBirth($0, in: calendar) })
        return save("About you") { [saveAboutYou] in try await saveAboutYou.execute(input) }

    /// Fills the name, age, and bedtime from the saved profile, unless a save is under way. A name being typed is kept,
    /// and a saved age outside the picker's range is left empty.
    private static func fillFields(_ state: inout State) {
        guard state.savesInFlight == 0, let profile = state.savedProfile else { return }
        if !state.isEditingName {
            state.name = profile.name ?? ""
        }
        state.bedtime = profile.bedtime
        if let year = state.currentYear {
            state.age = profile.birthYear.map { year - $0 }.flatMap { AboutYouFeature.ages.contains($0) ? $0 : nil }
        }
    }
```
- `new_string`:

```
    /// Fills the name, date of birth, and bedtime from the saved profile, unless a save is under way. A name being typed
    /// is kept, the date of birth waits for the picker's dates, and a saved date of birth the picker doesn't offer is
    /// left empty.
    private static func fillFields(_ state: inout State) {
        guard state.savesInFlight == 0, let profile = state.savedProfile else { return }
        if !state.isEditingName {
            state.name = profile.name ?? ""
        }
        state.bedtime = profile.bedtime
        if let picker = state.birthDatePicker {
            state.birthDate = picker.date(of: profile.dateOfBirth)
        }
    }
```

/// The factors come only from the profile the repository publishes, and an option saves at once (constitution Article
/// I.5). The name, date of birth, and bedtime are the sections' own editing state (Article I.2). They're refilled
/// from every published profile, except a name being typed, and except while a save is under way, so a picker being
/// changed never jumps back. The name saves when it's committed, and a newline typed in it commits it, because the
/// field wraps and Return types one. The date of birth, "Prefer not to say", and the bedtime save as soon as they're
/// chosen. A failed save puts the fields back to what was last published. See the Settings article, SETPROF-1 to
/// SETPROF-7.

12	import ComposableArchitecture
13	import SwiftUI

15	/// Settings' About you screen: the user's first name and age, as onboarding's About you step asks them.
16	///
17	/// The name field wraps, so Return types a newline, which commits the name, as leaving the field does. Once the name
18	/// is committed, the field lets go of the keyboard, which would otherwise stay up over the tab bar. The age is a
19	/// wheel, which passes the accessibility audit where iOS's menu picker didn't. See the Settings article.
20	@MainActor
21	struct AboutYouSettingsView: View {
22	    /// The screen's store.
23	    @Bindable var store: StoreOf<ProfileSettingsFeature>
24	
25	    @FocusState private var isNameFocused: Bool
26	
27	    /// The name field and the age wheel.
28	    var body: some View {
29	        SettingsScreen(title: Text("About you"), screenIdentifier: AboutYouSettingsViewAccessibilityID.screen) {
30	            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Spacing.sectionHeaderGap) {
31	                Text("First name")
32	                    .font(.footnote)
33	                    .foregroundStyle(Color.textPrimary)
34	                TextField(text: $store.name, prompt: Text("Your first name"), axis: .vertical) {
35	                    Text("First name")
36	                }
37	                .lineLimit(1...3)
38	                .textContentType(.givenName)
39	                .textInputAutocapitalization(.words)
40	                .autocorrectionDisabled()
41	                .submitLabel(.done)
42	                .focused($isNameFocused)
43	                .onSubmit { store.send(.nameCommitted) }
44	                .font(.body)
45	                .foregroundStyle(Color.textPrimary)
46	                .padding(.horizontal, Spacing.cardPaddingCompact)
47	                .padding(.vertical, Spacing.itemGap)
48	                .frame(minHeight: Sizing.chipHeight)
49	                .background(
50	                    Color.surfaceControl, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: CornerRadius.small, style: .continuous)
51	                )
```

//  _______  __   __  ___   ___      ___      _______  __    _  _______
// |       ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |   _   ||  |  | ||       |
// |   _   ||  | |  ||   | |   |    |   |    |  |_|  ||   |_| ||   _   |

// |____||_||_______||___| |_______||_______||__| |__||_|  |__||____||_|
//
// Half-Life BirthDateField
//

import SwiftUI

/// The date of birth, as onboarding's About you step and Settings' About you screen ask it: iOS's compact date picker
/// on a card, and a "Prefer not to say" option under it.
///
/// While "Prefer not to say" is chosen, the picker shows a 30-year-old's date of birth, and nothing is saved until the
/// user picks a date. The picker shows once its dates are known. See the Onboarding article, ONB-10.
@MainActor
struct BirthDateField: View {
    /// The chosen date of birth, or `nil` for "Prefer not to say".
    let birthDate: Date?
    /// The dates the picker offers, or `nil` until they're known.
    let picker: BirthDatePicker?
    /// The picker's accessibility identifier.
    let pickerIdentifier: String
    /// The "Prefer not to say" option's accessibility identifier.
    let preferNotToSayIdentifier: String
    /// What picking a date does.
    let choose: (Date) -> Void
    /// What choosing "Prefer not to say" does.
    let preferNotToSay: () -> Void

    /// The picker's card and the option.
    var body: some View {
        VStack(spacing: Spacing.itemGap) {
            if let picker {
                DatePicker(
                    selection: selection(suggesting: picker.suggestedDate), in: picker.dates,
                    displayedComponents: .date
                ) {
                    Text("Date of birth")
                        .font(.callout.weight(.semibold))
                        .foregroundStyle(Color.textPrimary)
                }
                .datePickerStyle(.compact)
                .padding(Spacing.cardPaddingCompact)
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: Sizing.minimumHitTarget)
                .background(
                    Color.surfaceCard, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: CornerRadius.medium, style: .continuous)
                )
                .accessibilityIdentifier(pickerIdentifier)
            }
            SettingsOption(
                title: Text("Prefer not to say"), detail: nil, isSelected: birthDate == nil,
                identifier: preferNotToSayIdentifier, action: preferNotToSay)
        }
    }

    /// The picker's date: the chosen date of birth, or the suggested one while none is chosen.
    private func selection(suggesting suggested: Date) -> Binding<Date> {
        Binding {
            birthDate ?? suggested
        } set: { date in
            choose(date)
        }
    }
}

```

== ONBOARDING
18:| Age | The recommended sleep range that sleep insights compare against | No |
31:3. **Age sets the recommended sleep range.** It doesn't change the half-life.
47:| 02: "What should we call you?" | Kept, and optional. Age joins it on one "About you" step. |
50:| 04: bedtime chips, 9:30pm to 12:00am | Replaced by a time picker for any time of day, so a shift worker's bedtime fits too. The owner chose this on 2026-09-12. |
67:| 2 | About you | Name (optional), age (optional) | On Continue |
69:| 4 | Bedtime | When they want to be asleep, on a time picker | On Continue |
84:- **Typed and picked answers are saved on Continue**: the name, the age, and the bedtime. They're the step's own editing state until then (Article I.2), so a half-typed name isn't saved. A step that's returned to fills in from the saved profile once, unless the user has already changed it.
99:| `birthYear` | `Int?` | `nil` | The year the user's age implies |
106:- **The age is stored as a birth year**, the current year minus the age given, so it doesn't go stale. It can be a year off, which matters only in the year a user crosses a sleep range's boundary. The owner chose this on 2026-09-12.
124:The nightly sleep recommended for the user's age.
131:When the age isn't known, it's the adult range. Nothing shows it yet except the summary. The Insights tab (rank 15) and insight cards (rank 12) will compare sleep against it.
152:| `SleepNeedRule` | The recommended sleep range, from the birth year and the current date | ``UserProfileRepository`` |
169:| Pregnant, first trimester | ×1.5 | 8.3 h | Caffeine clearance was 33% lower at 14–18 weeks than after the birth (Tracy 2005, 25 women followed through pregnancy). No primary study measured the first trimester itself. This is an upper bound, which errs toward an earlier cutoff. |
177:Each multiplier is a starting point for one person, drawn from group averages. Healthy people alone vary from about 2.3 to 9.9 hours (Blanchard 1983, 16 men), so the step and the summary call the result a starting estimate. The personal half-life estimator (rank 9) starts from it and updates it with the user's own nights. When the nights can't say anything, the estimate stays at this starting value (<doc:HalfLifeEstimator>).
183:| Age | No significant difference between men of 71 and 20 (Blanchard 1983). The owner decided the same on 2026-09-12. |
191:| Recently gave birth | Clearance returns to normal within days to a month. The sources disagree (Brazier 1983, Knutti 1981), and the answer would go stale quickly. |
194:#### SleepNeedRule
196:The recommended nightly sleep for the user's age, from the National Sleep Foundation's consensus (Hirshkowitz 2015).
198:| Age | Recommended | Also consistent with |
205:- Each boundary age belongs to the range it starts: 18 is an adult, and 65 is an older adult.
206:- The age is the current year minus the birth year, so it can be a year ahead of the user's real age.
207:- The youngest age the step accepts is 13. Younger children aren't the app's audience, and their sleep and caffeine guidance is different. The AI chose this, and it's still to be confirmed.
214:| ``SaveAboutYouUseCase`` | Saves the trimmed name, or none for a blank one, and the birth year the age implies in the given calendar | ``UserProfileRepository``, and ``CurrentTimeRepository`` for the year |
232:| ``UserProfileRepository`` (extended) | What the user has told the app: name, birth year, factors, and completion. It also writes the bedtime and the starting half-life, which ``CaffeineDecayRepository`` reads. | `FileProfileDataSource`, shared with ``CaffeineDecayRepository``; ``ClockDataSource`` |
263:| ``AboutYouFeature`` | Step 2: the name and age | ``ObserveUserProfileUseCase``, ``ObserveTimeOfDayUseCase`` (for the current year, to fill in the age), ``SaveAboutYouUseCase`` |
265:| ``BedtimeFeature`` | Step 4: the bedtime, on a time picker | ``ObserveUserProfileUseCase``, ``SaveBedtimeUseCase`` |
306:- **The Shortcuts button names the app from `CFBundleDisplayName`.** Without a display name, its label read " shortcuts". The app now declares `CFBundleDisplayName`, "Half-Life", in `InfoPlist.xcstrings`, with a placeholder in `Info.plist` (Article VII.1.1). UI-ONB-9 checks that the label names Half-Life.
318:- **The bedtime is two wheels**, hours and minutes, built from SwiftUI pickers, with hours in the locale's clock ("10 PM" or "22"). The system's time picker failed the audit's Dynamic Type check ("Dynamic Type font sizes are unsupported"). The age wheel, also a SwiftUI picker, passes.
333:| Birth year | The same file | `NSFileProtectionComplete` | The recommended sleep range |
346:- **Never logged, at any level:** the name, the age or birth year, any factor, the starting half-life, or the bedtime. The factors and the half-life are health values (XI.6.1). The name, age, and bedtime are personal data that no diagnosis needs (XI.6.2).
358:| How age is asked | A picker from 13 to 100, with "Prefer not to say", stored as a birth year |
359:| Bedtimes outside the prototype's chips | A time picker only, with no chips |
364:- **The bedtime saves on Continue.** With no card depending on it, it's editing state until Continue, like the name and age. A time picker that saved on every turn, and waited for the repository, could also have jumped back while turning.
